Desc_0
The food of Jingji includes lamb, soup, ribs, and buns of various types...
Desc_1
The culture of Capital includes drama, puppetry, novel, music, dance, Chinese wrestling, and shadow play...
Desc_2
The customs of Qixi needlework, colored banners, and the decorative melon craft all came from Jingji...
Desc_3
Due to the scarcity of vegetables in winter, the people of Capital need to stock up on enough winter greens before the Start of Winter...
Desc_4
A crimson bridge stretches like a rainbow over the river. Willows dip their branches into the waters. Green grass lines the embankments, where people often fish...
Desc_5
In the gardens of Jingji, one can occasionally glimpse a vision of the snow-covered forests of the Imperial Palace. Tourists from all over come here for a view of the stone ridges, streams and clouds...
Desc_6
The old palace either no longer exists, has collapsed, been rebuilt, or simply disappeared into thin air...
Desc_7
Jingji temples often preside over religious banquets and trade in rare items, books, household items, and more...
Desc_8
In the old days of Jingji, one could find glamorous carriages, precious horses, and many other lavish displays of wealth across the imperial roads...
Desc_9
The cuisine of Shu features Five Flavours. It's very spicy, and often supplemented by sugarcane, ginger, and pepper...
Desc_10
Bashu is rich in natural products such as jade and mushrooms, salt springs and iron veins, along with Ox Tail Banner...
Desc_11
Bashu has many entertainments. At the end of the season, many enjoy the talented singers and dancers, along with the sea of flowers in bloom...
Desc_12
The pandas of Shu are as large as bears. Black and white in color, they're docile creatures that mostly sustain themselves on bamboo...
Desc_13
The people of Bashu worship the White Tiger, meaning that sacrificial ceremonies are popular. One can occasionally find bronze trees and dummies in their sacrificial pits...
Desc_14
Li Bing once built embankments to guide the waters to control the floods. The lands of Shu flourished, and was touted as the heavenly kingdom...
Desc_15
The Emperor Wang Di, Du Yu, transformed into a large hawk cuckoo upon his death and called out. Since then, people reminisced about Emperor Wang Di whenever they saw this bird...
Desc_16
Yu the Great prospered in the Ba region. Later, he ruled the lands of the Yellow River from Bashu and conquered Jiuzhou...
Desc_17
There is a special type of coffin called the "hanging coffin." Placed in the holes of mountain walls, they remind one of a celestial burial...
Desc_18
There are people who live beneath the waterfall, rearing poultry and livestock beneath them in wooden pens, while they live above in so-called "dry fences"...
Desc_19
